A SACRAMENT CREATES AN OFFICE [claim EX29-01]. Immersion, vesting in order, anointing — and the priesthood declared an everlasting statute (Onkelos Exod 29): the machine's first personnel installation, a seven-day procedure whose output is an office. 		—Onkelos Exod 29witnessed
WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(ordination_frame, amnesty_announcement)
= WITNESS-READ(ordination-frame, amnesty-announcement)
THE PRIESTHOOD AS PUBLIC FORGIVENESS [claim EX29-10]. The spine reads the ordination as an amnesty made visible — bull for calf (the gold-for-gold pattern at the animal register, EX25-09's sibling), the rams for the sons, the offering owned and not stolen (Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 10). Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 10witnessed
Top split head mark L=etnachta (major mid-verse rest); R head mark noted in tree. maps_to this verse only.

FOR-ME MEANS FOREVER [claim EX29-11]. The token-law behind the everlasting renderings: every LI-grant in Scripture is perpetual, and the priesthood is on the census (Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 13) — the exclusivity and purity-curriculum clauses ride. Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 13witnessed

The leaning of hands on the offering's head appears here first, three times (Onkelos Exod 29) — with the body-map of blood on ear, thumb, and toe, and the garments sprinkled: the rite consecrates the person and the clothing together. 		—Onkelos Exod 29witnessed
